# FORMULA REGISTER — PRESERVED, PRE-RECONSTRUCTION

**This file is forensic evidence, not a working register.** It is the register as
it stood before the units reconstruction, kept whole so that the 190-unit figure
and every classification it carried remain historically traceable.

Its recorded output units are known to be wrong in **61 of 190** cases (F-38) and
its cells are truncated (F-39). Use `FORMULA-REGISTER.md` for current work and
`REGISTER-RECONSTRUCTION.md` for the change-by-change comparison.

---

# FORMULA REGISTER — classified, with Phase-0 metadata

Built by extraction from source. Every count here is measured; none is carried over from a
prior document.

## 1. What 2,429 actually is

**The answer is (C), a mixture — and the mixture is now separated.** 2,429 is the number of
IMPLEMENTATION SITES the extractor finds in the engineering modules. It is not the number of
engineering formulas, and this file no longer calls it that.

| kind | rows | share |
|---|---|---|
| **C1** distinct engineering relation | 1288 | 53.0% |
| **C7** duplicated implementation | 422 | 17.4% |
| **C5** geometric construction | 369 | 15.2% |
| **C3** intermediate arithmetic | 263 | 10.8% |
| **C4** unit conversion | 78 | 3.2% |
| **C6** UI/display calculation | 9 | 0.4% |
| **total sites** | **2429** | 100% |

**Distinct engineering relations: 1288 retained sites, 1193 distinct expression shapes among them.**

The two numbers differ because C1 counts the first occurrence of a shape *per file*: a relation
written twice inside one module is two C1 rows. So the number of distinct engineering relations
is **at most 1193**, and the honest statement is a bracket, not a point:

> between 1193 and 1288 distinct engineering relations, implemented at 2429 sites,
> of which 422 are duplicate implementations of a relation that already exists elsewhere.

For comparison, `FORMULA-INVENTORY.md` reports 147. That census counted the MAJOR relations and
said so. The gap between 147 and 1288 is the Phase-2 backlog, not a contradiction.

## 2. The classification rule

Applied in this order; **first match wins**, so the order is the rule and every row lands in
exactly one class.

| order | class | rule |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **C6 UI/display** | Computes something for looking at: pixel or viewport scaling, chart coordinates, colour, label placement. Detected by a view/scene/projection module or a screen-quantity name (px, scale, viewBox, stroke, opacity, tick). |
| 2 | **C5 geometric construction** | Builds coordinates, meshes, rotations or polygons — a SHAPE rather than a physical relation. Detected by module (geometry, mesh, scene3d, projection, outline, boom) or by the line placing/rotating a point. Lines that also carry mass, thrust, power, drag, lift, energy or fuel are exempted and fall through to C1. |
| 3 | **C4 unit conversion** | Multiplies or divides by a conversion factor and little else: a known factor literal (2.20462, 3.28084, 0.3048, 1.852, 0.7457, 47.8803, …) or a named unit constant (LB, FT, KW_PER_HP, …), with at most three operators on the line. |
| 4 | **C3 intermediate arithmetic** | Algebraic bookkeeping with no independent physical content: a temporary name (single letter, t/tmp/acc/sum/num/den/k1/f0…), or a whole right-hand side that is only max/min/abs/round of one term. |
| 5 | **C7 duplicated implementation** | The identifier-folded expression shape occurs in more than one FILE and this is not the first occurrence in file order. The first occurrence stays C1 and carries the relation. |
| 6 | **C1 distinct engineering relation** | What remains: a relation between named physical quantities at its first implementation site in that file. |

**C2 'implementation site' is deliberately not a class.** Every row is an implementation site —
that is the unit the extractor produces. The question "is 2,429 relations or sites?" is answered
by the C1 count against the total, and by C7 for the duplicates, not by a class of its own.

### What the rule gets wrong

It is pattern-based, so it misclassifies at the edges: a relation assigned to a short variable
name lands in C3, and a genuine aerodynamic relation inside `geometry.js` can land in C5 unless
it names a force or a mass. Every row carries its class in the tables below, so a misclassified
row is visible and correctable rather than hidden inside a total.

## 3. Metadata coverage, stated honestly

| field | P00 | filled | how |
|---|---|---|---|
| Formula ID | 035/043 | **2429 / 2429** | assigned, stable, unique |
| File | 044 | **2429 / 2429** | extraction |
| Function | 045 | **2429 / 2429** | nearest enclosing declaration |
| Line | 046 | **2429 / 2429** | extraction, verified against raw source |
| Equation | 047 | **2429 / 2429** | source line verbatim |
| Duplicated elsewhere | 054 | **2429 / 2429** | cross-file shape match |
| Configuration-specific | 055 | **2429 / 2429** | module path |
| Current tests | 057 | **2429 / 2429** | harnesses importing the file (FILE-level) |
| Variable definitions | 048 | **1288 / 1288** | identifiers on the right-hand side |
| Expected units per variable | 049 | **908 / 5055 (18.0%)** | naming convention or a stated comment; else UNKNOWN |
| Output units | 050 | **190 / 1288 (14.8%)** | trailing comment first, then naming convention; else UNKNOWN |
| Reference/source | 051 | **1288 / 1288** | nearest tag or citation; else UNVERIFIED |
| Source type | 052 | **1288 / 1288** | from the tag; else UNKNOWN |
| Independently validated | 053 | **1288 / 1288** | recorded NO for all — see below |
| Known limitations | 056 | **0 / 1288** | UNKNOWN for all — see below |
| Audit status | 058 | **1288 / 1288** | NOT AUDITED — Phase 2 |

**Three fields are recorded as absent for every relation, and they are absent for a reason, not
by oversight:**

- **Independently validated — NO for all 1288.** Phase 3 recomputes roughly 20 quantities across
  three reference scripts, so at most ~20 rows could ever say YES, and which 20 is not recorded
  per formula. Reading the census's "113 PASS" as independent validation would be exactly the
  inference this audit forbids: a domain verdict is not a per-formula recomputation.
- **Known limitations — UNKNOWN for all 1288.** The instruction is explicit that generic text is
  not acceptable here. A real validity limitation (Howe's `AR > 5, M < 0.95`; the EMRAX torque
  ceiling; AFDD's fitted mass range) exists for some relations and is written in the module
  headers, but attaching the right limitation to the right line requires reading each relation.
  UNKNOWN is the truthful entry until that is done.
- **Output units — UNKNOWN for 1098 of 1288 (85.2%).** This is a finding about the code, not a gap
  in the method: **most computed quantities in this tree do not state their units**, by neither
  naming convention nor comment. Phase 1 is the unit-system audit and this is its backlog.
